# Evidence behind REVIEW.md

Generated by `build_evidence.py` from each run's decision records. Locators such as `S2.5.p1` point to a section and paragraph of the paper's structured text; `FIG2` to a figure legend.

## 1. GSE283493 — 

- **Paper:** [PMID 39832562](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/39832562/); **GEO:** [GSE283493](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/query/acc.cgi?acc=GSE283493)
- **Status:** finished; validator: VALID (50 decision records in 5 file(s)); 8.6 min, 190945 tokens
- **Comparison with the paper:** 4/5 cases; Wald DEGs 0/497/2/37 vs paper 7/353/2/14; LRT stopped (no animal ID, no LRT executor)

#### Case `interaction_lrt`

- **Paper's estimand:** Genes whose exercise response differs between ZT13 and ZT23 (phase:training interaction), modelled across SAT and VAT with tissue as an additive term
- **Paper's planned contrasts:** [{"name": "phase:training interaction across tissues", "test": "DESeq2 LRT: SVs + Tissue + Phase + Training + Phase:Training vs reduced without interaction", "locator": "S2.9.p2"}]
- **Paper's threshold:** padj < 0.1 (not_reported) — paper S2.9.p2; FIG1
- **Paper's method:** DESeq2 (Wald + apeglm; LRT for interaction) with 2 SVs
- **Matrix:** raw counts
- **Design:** factorial; formula `~ tissue + phase + training + phase:training`
- **Covariance:** unresolved; blocking none; covariates [{"name": "animal", "disposition": "unresolved: SAT and VAT come from the same rats (S2.5.p1, S2.9.p1) but no animal ID is deposited; replicate letters a-f are not documented as animal IDs"}, {"name": …
- **Stopped at `covariance` (manual_review):** Model pools SAT and VAT from the same rats, so animal is a repeated-measure key, but GEO has no animal ID (replicate letters cannot be verified as rats). In addition no DESeq2 LRT executor exists. Unblock with an animal-to-library table from the authors and an LRT or limma interaction route.

  - {"item": "surrogate variables", "paper": "2 SVs (sva on top 3000 variable genes) added to every DESeq2 model (S2.9.p2)", "data": "no sva executor in the repository", "disposition": "requires_explicit_deviation", "resolution": "Wald contrasts run without SVs as a declared deviation"}

## 2. GSE208639 — 

- **Paper:** [PMID 37318869](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/37318869/); **GEO:** [GSE208639](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/query/acc.cgi?acc=GSE208639)
- **Status:** finished; validator: VALID (33 decision records in 3 file(s)); 16.0 min, 206759 tokens
- **Comparison with the paper:** 3/3 cases; post vs pre 20 DEGs (ECM up, matches paper); pre vs ctrl 475 vs authors 176 (no TMM at the time)

#### Case `case_01`

- **Paper's estimand:** Effect of 12-week strength training on muscle gene expression in DM1, quantified as the change from pre- to post-training relative to unaffected controls (Δlog2FC = log2FC[post vs control] - log2FC[pre vs control]) and as % rescue of pre-training DEGs; analysed grouped and per individual (S2.2.p2, S2.5.p1, S4.6.p1)
- **Paper's planned contrasts:** [{"name": "grouped_pre_vs_control", "locator": "S2.2.p2, S4.13.p1", "test": "edgeR QL F-test"}, {"name": "grouped_post_vs_control", "locator": "S2.2.p2, S4.13.p1", "test": "edgeR QL F-test"}, {"name": "grouped_delta_log2FC (post-vs-control minus pre-vs-control)", "locator": "S2.2.p2", "test": "none (difference of two fitted log2FCs; no p-value)"}, {"name": "individual_<id>_pre_vs_control and indiv …
- **Paper's threshold:** pvalue < 0.05 |log2FC| > 2.0 (none) — paper S4.4.p1
- **Paper's method:** edgeR 3.34.1, quasi-likelihood F-test, each group vs unaffected controls (S4.4.p1, S4.13.p1)
- **Matrix:** raw counts
- **Design:** paired (two timepoints within subject); formula `~ subject + grp`
- **Covariance:** required; blocking ['subject']; covariates [{"name": "subject", "disposition": "included"}, {"name": "age", "disposition": "excluded: not in GEO metadata; constant within subject anyway"}, {"name": "sex", "disposition": "excluded: single level …
- **Method:** DESeq2 (pydeseq2), Wald test — paper route: {"method": "edgeR 3.34.1 QL F-test, each group vs controls, Δlog2FC", "status": "blocked", "reason": "paper has no post-vs-pre test; repository edgeR executor (tools/edger_tools.py) fits only ~ Treatment, no subject blocking, and no TMM. Paper's grou …
- **Applied threshold:** pvalue < 0.05 |log2FC| > 2.0 (none) (source: paper_on_deviation_route)
- **Result tables (on the PC, not in git):** `PMID37318869/case_01/results/deseq2_paired/DEG_results_post_vs_pre.csv`
- **Route:** declared_deviation

## 6. GSE152133 — Skeletal Muscle Transcriptome Alterations Related to Declining Physical Function in Older Mice

- **Paper:** [PMID 37876943](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/37876943/); **GEO:** [GSE152133](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/query/acc.cgi?acc=GSE152133)
- **Status:** refused_design; validator: VALID (5 decision records in 1 file(s)); 2.7 min, 113794 tokens
- **Comparison with the paper:** aging study (6/24/28 mo), no exercise intervention; correctly refused at design gate; screen false positive

#### Case `case_01`

- **Paper's estimand:** age effect on TA gene expression (28m vs 6m within run2; 24m vs 6m within run1), then association of DEG expression with CFAB functional score (S3.2.p2)
- **Paper's planned contrasts:** [{"name": "28m_vs_6m", "locator": "S4.1.1.1.p1", "paper_counts": {"up": 605, "down": 612}}, {"name": "24m_vs_6m", "locator": "S4.1.1.2.p1", "paper_counts": {"up": 130, "down": 8}}, {"name": "DEG-vs-CFAB linear regression", "locator": "S3.2.p2"}]
- **Paper's threshold:** padj ≤ 0.05 |log2FoldChange| ≥ 1.0 (BH (DESeq2 default)) — paper S3.1.1.p2
- **Matrix:** raw counts
- **Stopped at `design` (refused):** no exercise contrast in the sequenced samples: GSE152133 is a cross-sectional aging study (male C57BL/6 TA muscle at 6, 24, 28 months); exercise appears only as part of the CFAB functional test battery given to every mouse (S2.2.p1) and in discussion of external datasets (S5.8.p2)
- **Paper vs data discrepancies (2):**
  - {"item": "requested estimand", "paper": "aging (6m vs 24m/28m) and DEG~CFAB association; no exercise intervention", "data": "factors are age (m6/m24/m28) and sequencing run only", "consequence": "exercise effect not identifiable"}
  - {"item": "library count", "paper": "22 RNA samples, 6m libraries prepared twice (S2.4.p1)", "data": "29 GSMs = 7 m6 run1 + 7 m6 run2 (_repeat) + 7 m24 run1 + 8 m28 run2", "consequence": "consistent"}

## 7. GSE179302 — Physical Activity Associates with Lower Systemic Inflammatory Gene Expression in Rheumatoid Arthritis

- **Paper:** [PMID 35777820](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/35777820/); **GEO:** [GSE179302](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/query/acc.cgi?acc=GSE179302)
- **Status:** finished_not_reproduced; validator: VALID (19 decision records in 1 file(s)); 16.7 min, 155010 tokens

- **Status:** refused_design; validator: VALID (5 decision records in 1 file(s)); 6.2 min, 159817 tokens
- **Comparison with the paper:** GSE188236 is the in-vitro TGF-b myotube arm (no exercise samples); the paper's training data are on microarray series GSE72462/GSE161749; correctly refused at design; screen false positive

#### Case `GSE188236`

- **Paper's estimand:** Effect of TGF-beta1 (and its reversal by ALK4/5/7 inhibition) on long-RNA and miRNA expression in differentiating human myotubes (S3.1.p1). The paper's exercise estimand (training response, low responders vs responders, pre vs post 8-week training) is measured in GSE72462/GSE161749 microarrays and by qPCR, not in GSE188236 (S2.5.p1, S3.3.p1).
- **Paper's planned contrasts:** ["TGF-beta1 vs vehicle control (long RNA; 2066 transcripts)", "TGF-beta1 + SB431542 vs TGF-beta1 (long RNA; 1900 transcripts)", "TGF-beta1 vs vehicle control (small RNA classical; 45 miRNAs)"]

- **Paper's method:** not_reported for the RNA-seq contrasts (S2.6.p1 describes limma for the arrays; GEO says DESeq2 normalization)
- **Matrix:** normalized counts (DESeq2 size-factor normalized; not raw counts)
- **Design:** ; formula `None`; suitability: analyzable
- **Stopped at `design` (refused):** GSE188236 contains only cultured primary human myotubes treated with vehicle, TGF-beta1, or TGF-beta1+SB431542 (in-vitro arm of the paper, S2.4.p1/S3.1.p1). It has no exercise, training, or responder factor, so it cannot identify how exercise affects gene expression. The paper's exercise analysis (8-week endurance training, pre/post biopsies, responders vs low responders) uses microarrays GSE72462 and GSE161749 (S2.5.p1), outside this request.

## 35. GSE304653 — Exercise suppresses breast cancer and reprograms the immune tumor microenvironment, cancer cell intrinsic features, and their interaction.

- **Paper:** [PMID 42155766](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42155766/); **GEO:** [GSE304653](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/query/acc.cgi?acc=GSE304653)
- **Status:** finished; validator: VALID (22 decision records in 2 file(s)); 8.0 min, 166399 tokens

## 39. GSE326247 — Skeletal Muscle-Derived Extracellular Vesicles During Eccentric and Concentric Exercise Promote Muscle Regeneration After Injury.

- **Paper:** [PMID 42666019](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42666019/); **GEO:** [GSE326247](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/query/acc.cgi?acc=GSE326247)
- **Status:** finished_mediator; validator: VALID (16 decision records in 1 file(s)); 7.3 min, 175062 tokens
- **Comparison with the paper:** C2C12 myoblasts treated with EVs from sedentary / concentric / eccentric exercised muscle (cell-level proxy, no exercised tissue); joint DESeq2, padj<0.05 & |log2FC|>1: CE vs SED 834, EE vs CE 47 (paper: volcano only); OXPHOS genes up CE vs SED as in paper, not further up in EE

#### Case `PMID42666019`

- **Paper's estimand:** Transcriptomic effect on C2C12 myoblasts of muscle-derived EVs from exercised mice (concentric CE, eccentric EE) versus EVs from sedentary mice, and EE- versus CE-derived EVs. Exercise is the treatment of the EV-donor mice; the sequenced cells were not exercised.
- **Paper's planned contrasts:** ["grp_CE_vs_SED", "grp_EE_vs_CE"]
